      --header 'POLYMARKET-PROXY: <api-key>' \
      --header 'POLYMARKET-SECRET: <api-key>'

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/account/portfolio"
    
    headers = {
        "POLYMARKET-PROXY": "<api-key>",
        "POLYMARKET-SECRET": "<api-key>"
    }
    
    response = requests.get(url, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

      method: 'GET',
      headers: {'POLYMARKET-PROXY': '<api-key>', 'POLYMARKET-SECRET': '<api-key>'}
    };

      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "GET",\
      CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => [\
        "POLYMARKET-PROXY: <api-key>",\

    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);

      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

Modify Orders

cURL

    curl --request PATCH \
      --url https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/orders \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '
    {
      "op": {
        "type": "modifyOrders",
        "args": [\
          {\
            "oid": 1234567890,\
            "p": "100.00",\
            "qty": "10.00"\
          }\
        ]
      },
      "sig": "0x1234567890...",
      "salt": 1234567890,
      "ts": 1767225600000,
      "exp": 1767225600000
    }
    '

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/orders"
    
    payload = {
        "op": {
            "type": "modifyOrders",
            "args": [\
                {\
                    "oid": 1234567890,\
                    "p": "100.00",\
                    "qty": "10.00"\
                }\
            ]
        },
        "sig": "0x1234567890...",
        "salt": 1234567890,
        "ts": 1767225600000,
        "exp": 1767225600000
    }
    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
    
    response = requests.patch(url, json=payload, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({
        op: {type: 'modifyOrders', args: [{oid: 1234567890, p: '100.00', qty: '10.00'}]},
        sig: '0x1234567890...',
        salt: 1234567890,
        ts: 1767225600000,
        exp: 1767225600000
      })
    };
    
    fetch('https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/orders', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/orders",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "PATCH",\
      CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS => json_encode([\
        'op' => [\
            'type' => 'modifyOrders',\
            'args' => [\
                    [\
                                    'oid' => 1234567890,\
                                    'p' => '100.00',\
                                    'qty' => '10.00'\
                    ]\
            ]\
        ],\
        'sig' => '0x1234567890...',\
        'salt' => 1234567890,\
        'ts' => 1767225600000,\
        'exp' => 1767225600000\
      ]),\
      CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => [\
        "Content-Type: application/json"\
      ],\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"strings"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/orders"
    
    	payload := strings.NewReader("{\n  \"op\": {\n    \"type\": \"modifyOrders\",\n    \"args\": [\n      {\n        \"oid\": 1234567890,\n        \"p\": \"100.00\",\n        \"qty\": \"10.00\"\n      }\n    ]\n  },\n  \"sig\": \"0x1234567890...\",\n  \"salt\": 1234567890,\n  \"ts\": 1767225600000,\n  \"exp\": 1767225600000\n}")
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("PATCH", url, payload)
    
    	req.Header.Add("Content-Type", "application/json")
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.patch("https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/orders")
      .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
      .body("{\n  \"op\": {\n    \"type\": \"modifyOrders\",\n    \"args\": [\n      {\n        \"oid\": 1234567890,\n        \"p\": \"100.00\",\n        \"qty\": \"10.00\"\n      }\n    ]\n  },\n  \"sig\": \"0x1234567890...\",\n  \"salt\": 1234567890,\n  \"ts\": 1767225600000,\n  \"exp\": 1767225600000\n}")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'

    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

Modify Orders

cURL

    curl --request PATCH \
      --url https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/orders \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '
    {
      "op": {
        "type": "modifyOrders",
        "args": [\
          {\
            "oid": 1234567890,\
            "p": "100.00",\
            "qty": "10.00"\
          }\
        ]
      },
      "sig": "0x1234567890...",
      "salt": 1234567890,
      "ts": 1767225600000,
      "exp": 1767225600000
    }
    '

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/orders"
    
    payload = {
        "op": {
            "type": "modifyOrders",
            "args": [\
                {\
                    "oid": 1234567890,\
                    "p": "100.00",\
                    "qty": "10.00"\
                }\
            ]
        },
        "sig": "0x1234567890...",
        "salt": 1234567890,
        "ts": 1767225600000,
        "exp": 1767225600000
    }
    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
    
    response = requests.patch(url, json=payload, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({
        op: {type: 'modifyOrders', args: [{oid: 1234567890, p: '100.00', qty: '10.00'}]},
        sig: '0x1234567890...',
        salt: 1234567890,
        ts: 1767225600000,
        exp: 1767225600000
      })
    };
    
    fetch('https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/orders', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/orders",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "PATCH",\
      CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS => json_encode([\
        'op' => [\
            'type' => 'modifyOrders',\
            'args' => [\
                    [\
                                    'oid' => 1234567890,\
                                    'p' => '100.00',\
                                    'qty' => '10.00'\
                    ]\
            ]\
        ],\
        'sig' => '0x1234567890...',\
        'salt' => 1234567890,\
        'ts' => 1767225600000,\
        'exp' => 1767225600000\
      ]),\
      CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => [\
        "Content-Type: application/json"\
      ],\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"strings"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/orders"
    
    	payload := strings.NewReader("{\n  \"op\": {\n    \"type\": \"modifyOrders\",\n    \"args\": [\n      {\n        \"oid\": 1234567890,\n        \"p\": \"100.00\",\n        \"qty\": \"10.00\"\n      }\n    ]\n  },\n  \"sig\": \"0x1234567890...\",\n  \"salt\": 1234567890,\n  \"ts\": 1767225600000,\n  \"exp\": 1767225600000\n}")
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("PATCH", url, payload)
    
    	req.Header.Add("Content-Type", "application/json")
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.patch("https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/orders")
      .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
      .body("{\n  \"op\": {\n    \"type\": \"modifyOrders\",\n    \"args\": [\n      {\n        \"oid\": 1234567890,\n        \"p\": \"100.00\",\n        \"qty\": \"10.00\"\n      }\n    ]\n  },\n  \"sig\": \"0x1234567890...\",\n  \"salt\": 1234567890,\n  \"ts\": 1767225600000,\n  \"exp\": 1767225600000\n}")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/orders")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Patch.new(url)
    request["Content-Type"] = 'application/json'
    request.body = "{\n  \"op\": {\n    \"type\": \"modifyOrders\",\n    \"args\": [\n      {\n        \"oid\": 1234567890,\n        \"p\": \"100.00\",\n        \"qty\": \"10.00\"\n      }\n    ]\n  },\n  \"sig\": \"0x1234567890...\",\n  \"salt\": 1234567890,\n  \"ts\": 1767225600000,\n  \"exp\": 1767225600000\n}"
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

Request Weight: **1 + floor(n / 20)** Action Weight: **1 / order**

#### Body

application/json

Modify request.

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/modify-orders#body-op)

op

object

required

Show child attributes

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/modify-orders#body-sig)

sig

string

required

Signature in hex format

Example:

`"0x1234567890..."`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/modify-orders#body-salt)

salt

integer

required

Salt

Example:

`1234567890`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/modify-orders#body-ts)

ts

integer

required

Request timestamp. Unix milliseconds for most operations; Unix seconds for withdrawals (must match the on-chain EIP-712 struct verified against block.timestamp).

Example:

`1767225600000`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/modify-orders#body-exp)

exp

integer

Command expiry timestamp in Unix milliseconds. If provided, it must be in the future and within the gateway's default command timeout. It can shorten request validity but cannot extend it. This is not an order auto-cancel time.

Example:

`1767225600000`

#### Response

200

application/json

One modify result per requested order, in request order.

*   Option 1
    
*   Option 2
    

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/modify-orders#response-items-one-of-0-status)

status

enum<string>

required

Available options:

`ok`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/modify-orders#response-items-one-of-0-order)

order

object

required

Show child attributes

Update Leverage for Multiple Instruments

cURL

    curl --request PATCH \
      --url https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/leverage/batch \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '
    {
      "op": {
        "type": "updateLeverages",
        "args": [\
          {\
            "iid": 1,\
            "lev": 5,\
            "cross": false\
          },\
          {\
            "iid": 2,\
            "lev": 10,\
            "cross": true\
          }\
        ]
      },
      "sig": "0x59ed57f6dce8d15aa01e774ef53d9957c84801fb34ec655f6a2ea344af8a58843095314730a3f1bd8f0f4560f6dc6f8de69d3f2d0a6f3365fc877f7f4845d40b1c",
      "salt": 333333333,
      "ts": 1767000012000
    }
    '

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/leverage/batch"

            "type": "updateLeverages",

                {\
                    "iid": 1,\
                    "lev": 5,\
                    "cross": False\
                },\
                {\
                    "iid": 2,\
                    "lev": 10,\
                    "cross": True\

            ]
        },
        "sig": "0x59ed57f6dce8d15aa01e774ef53d9957c84801fb34ec655f6a2ea344af8a58843095314730a3f1bd8f0f4560f6dc6f8de69d3f2d0a6f3365fc877f7f4845d40b1c",
        "salt": 333333333,
        "ts": 1767000012000
    }
    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
    
    response = requests.patch(url, json=payload, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({
        op: {
          type: 'updateLeverages',
          args: [{iid: 1, lev: 5, cross: false}, {iid: 2, lev: 10, cross: true}]
        },
        sig: '0x59ed57f6dce8d15aa01e774ef53d9957c84801fb34ec655f6a2ea344af8a58843095314730a3f1bd8f0f4560f6dc6f8de69d3f2d0a6f3365fc877f7f4845d40b1c',
        salt: 333333333,
        ts: 1767000012000

    fetch('https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/leverage/batch', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

      .body("{\n  \"op\": {\n    \"type\": \"updateLeverages\",\n    \"args\": [\n      {\n        \"iid\": 1,\n        \"lev\": 5,\n        \"cross\": false\n      },\n      {\n        \"iid\": 2,\n        \"lev\": 10,\n        \"cross\": true\n      }\n    ]\n  },\n  \"sig\": \"0x59ed57f6dce8d15aa01e774ef53d9957c84801fb34ec655f6a2ea344af8a58843095314730a3f1bd8f0f4560f6dc6f8de69d3f2d0a6f3365fc877f7f4845d40b1c\",\n  \"salt\": 333333333,\n  \"ts\": 1767000012000\n}")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/trade/leverage/batch")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Patch.new(url)
    request["Content-Type"] = 'application/json'
    request.body = "{\n  \"op\": {\n    \"type\": \"updateLeverages\",\n    \"args\": [\n      {\n        \"iid\": 1,\n        \"lev\": 5,\n        \"cross\": false\n      },\n      {\n        \"iid\": 2,\n        \"lev\": 10,\n        \"cross\": true\n      }\n    ]\n  },\n  \"sig\": \"0x59ed57f6dce8d15aa01e774ef53d9957c84801fb34ec655f6a2ea344af8a58843095314730a3f1bd8f0f4560f6dc6f8de69d3f2d0a6f3365fc877f7f4845d40b1c\",\n  \"salt\": 333333333,\n  \"ts\": 1767000012000\n}"
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

Request Weight: **1 + floor(n / 20)** Action Weight: **1 / instrument**

#### Body

application/json

Batch leverage request.

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/update-leverage-for-multiple-instruments#body-op)

op

object

required

Show child attributes

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/update-leverage-for-multiple-instruments#body-sig)

sig

string

required

Signature in hex format

Example:

`"0x1234567890..."`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/update-leverage-for-multiple-instruments#body-salt)

salt

integer

required

Salt

Example:

`1234567890`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/update-leverage-for-multiple-instruments#body-ts)

ts

integer

required

Request timestamp. Unix milliseconds for most operations; Unix seconds for withdrawals (must match the on-chain EIP-712 struct verified against block.timestamp).

Example:

`1767225600000`

#### Response

200

application/json

Ordered result for each requested instrument.

*   Option 1
    
*   Option 2
    

The instrument's effective leverage configuration after the update.

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/update-leverage-for-multiple-instruments#response-items-one-of-0-status)

status

enum<string>

required

Available options:

`ok`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/update-leverage-for-multiple-instruments#response-items-one-of-0-instrument-id)

instrument\_id

integer

required

Instrument ID

Example:

`1`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/update-leverage-for-multiple-instruments#response-items-one-of-0-leverage)

leverage

integer

required

Leverage

Example:

`10`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/update-leverage-for-multiple-instruments#response-items-one-of-0-cross)

cross

boolean

required

Whether to use cross margin mode

# Get Exchange Statistics - Polymarket Documentation

> Documentation Index

> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/llms.txt](https://docs.polymarket.com/llms.txt)

[Skip to main content](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-exchange-statistics#content-area)

Get Exchange Statistics

cURL

    curl --request GET \
      --url https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/info/exchange-stats

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/info/exchange-stats"
    
    response = requests.get(url)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {method: 'GET'};
    
    fetch('https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/info/exchange-stats', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/info/exchange-stats",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "GET",\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/info/exchange-stats"
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("GET", url, nil)
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.get("https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/info/exchange-stats")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/info/exchange-stats")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Get.new(url)
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-exchange-statistics#parameter-start-timestamp)

start\_timestamp

integer

required

Start timestamp in milliseconds

Example:

`1767225600000`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-exchange-statistics#parameter-end-timestamp)

end\_timestamp

integer

required

End timestamp in milliseconds

Example:

`1767229200000`

#### Response

200

application/json

Exchange statistics response.

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-exchange-statistics#response-start-timestamp)

start\_timestamp

integer

required

Start timestamp in milliseconds

Example:

`1767225600000`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-exchange-statistics#response-end-timestamp)

end\_timestamp

integer

required

End timestamp in milliseconds

Example:

`1767229200000`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-exchange-statistics#response-volume)

volume

string

required

Matched trading volume in USD notional for the requested window, as an unformatted numeric string

Example:

`"10164976.25"`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-exchange-statistics#response-open-interest-one-of-0)

open\_interest

string | null

required

One-sided open interest in USD notional at the latest complete sample before the requested window end, as an unformatted numeric string. Null when no complete sample is available.

Example:

`"9238345.12"`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-exchange-statistics#response-open-interest-timestamp-one-of-0)

open\_interest\_timestamp

integer | null

required

Timestamp of the open-interest sample in Unix milliseconds. Null when no complete sample is available.

Example:

`1767225570000`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-exchange-statistics#response-fees)

fees

string

required

Gross positive maker and taker trading fees in USD for the requested window, as an unformatted numeric string. Rebates, incentives, and referral payments are excluded.

Example:

`"1245.67"`

enum<string>

Available options:

`perpetual`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-instruments#parameter-category)

category

enum<string>

Available options:

`equity`,

`commodity`,

`index`,

`crypto`

#### Response

200

application/json

Instruments response.

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-instruments#response-items-instrument-id)

instrument\_id

integer

required

Instrument ID

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-instruments#response-items-instrument-type)

enum<string>

required

Instrument type

Available options:

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-instruments#response-items-category)

category

enum<string>

required

Instrument category

Available options:

`equity`,

`commodity`,

`index`,

`crypto`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-instruments#response-items-isolated-only)

isolated\_only

boolean

required

Whether the instrument supports only isolated account margin. When false, both cross and isolated account margin modes are supported.

string

required

Instrument symbol

integer

required

Number of decimal places for price. Non-integer prices have a maximum of 5 significant figures; integer prices are allowed regardless of significant figures.

`2`

required

max\_order\_count

Maximum limit order notional value in USD

`"10000000.00"`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-instruments#response-items-max-leverage)

max\_leverage

integer

required

Maximum leverage

Example:

`20`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-instruments#response-items-risk-tiers)

risk\_tiers

object\[\]

required

Show child attributes

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-instruments#response-items-ui-live-time-one-of-0)

ui\_live\_time

integer | null

required

Unix timestamp in milliseconds when first-party interfaces may display the instrument. A null value means hidden. This advisory field does not affect trading availability.

Required range: `0 <= x <= 253402300799000`

Example:

`1786032000000`

Get Open Orders

cURL

    curl --request GET \
      --url https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/account/open-orders \
      --header 'POLYMARKET-PROXY: <api-key>' \
      --header 'POLYMARKET-SECRET: <api-key>'

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/account/open-orders"
    
    headers = {
        "POLYMARKET-PROXY": "<api-key>",
        "POLYMARKET-SECRET": "<api-key>"
    }
    
    response = requests.get(url, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {
      method: 'GET',
      headers: {'POLYMARKET-PROXY': '<api-key>', 'POLYMARKET-SECRET': '<api-key>'}
    };
    
    fetch('https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/account/open-orders', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/account/open-orders",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "GET",\
      CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => [\
        "POLYMARKET-PROXY: <api-key>",\
        "POLYMARKET-SECRET: <api-key>"\
      ],\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/account/open-orders"
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("GET", url, nil)
    
    	req.Header.Add("POLYMARKET-PROXY", "<api-key>")
    	req.Header.Add("POLYMARKET-SECRET", "<api-key>")
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.get("https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/account/open-orders")
      .header("POLYMARKET-PROXY", "<api-key>")
      .header("POLYMARKET-SECRET", "<api-key>")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/account/open-orders")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Get.new(url)
    request["POLYMARKET-PROXY"] = '<api-key>'
    request["POLYMARKET-SECRET"] = '<api-key>'
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

# Overview - Polymarket Documentation

> Documentation Index
> -------------------
> 
> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/llms.txt](https://docs.polymarket.com/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/perps/overview#content-area)

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/perps/overview#apis)

APIs
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

The Perps API is available over HTTP and WebSocket, with public market data and authenticated trading and account operations on both.

HTTP API
--------

`[https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com](https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/) `Make request-response calls for market data, trading, and account management.

WebSocket API
-------------

`wss://ws.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/ws`Stream public and private updates, or send trading actions over a persistent connection.

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/perps/overview#before-you-integrate)

Before You Integrate
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Authentication
--------------

Set up credentials for private HTTP and WebSocket access.

Rate Limits
-----------

Review request, trading action, and WebSocket limits.

Geographic Restrictions
-----------------------

Check where Perps order placement is available.

Was this page helpful?

YesNo

---

# Geographic Restrictions - Polymarket Documentation

> Documentation Index
> -------------------
> 
> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/llms.txt](https://docs.polymarket.com/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/perps/geographic-restrictions#content-area)

Polymarket restricts order placement from certain geographic locations to comply with regulatory requirements and international sanctions. Users in restricted jurisdictions cannot place Perps orders.

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/perps/geographic-restrictions#restricted-jurisdictions)

Restricted Jurisdictions
---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Order placement is not permitted from:

*   United States
*   Canada
*   Cuba
*   Iran
*   North Korea
*   Syria
*   Crimea
*   Donetsk
*   Luhansk

This list can change. Additional restrictions may apply under Polymarket notices or applicable law.

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/perps/geographic-restrictions#for-builders)

For Builders
---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

If you’re integrating Perps, enforce these restrictions before submitting orders for a user:

*   Verify the end user’s location before [placing orders](https://docs.polymarket.com/perps/trading#place-orders)
    .
*   Block order submission entirely for users in any of the listed jurisdictions. Do not only display a warning.
*   Apply the same check to any flow that results in a new position, including programmatic strategies that act on behalf of a user.

Read-only market data is not subject to these restrictions.

Was this page helpful?

YesNo

    curl --request GET \
      --url https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/account/orders \
      --header 'POLYMARKET-PROXY: <api-key>' \
      --header 'POLYMARKET-SECRET: <api-key>'

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/account/orders"
    
    headers = {
        "POLYMARKET-PROXY": "<api-key>",
        "POLYMARKET-SECRET": "<api-key>"
    }
    
    response = requests.get(url, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {
      method: 'GET',
      headers: {'POLYMARKET-PROXY': '<api-key>', 'POLYMARKET-SECRET': '<api-key>'}
    };
    
    fetch('https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/account/orders', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/account/orders",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "GET",\
      CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => [\
        "POLYMARKET-PROXY: <api-key>",\
        "POLYMARKET-SECRET: <api-key>"\
      ],\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/account/orders"
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("GET", url, nil)
    
    	req.Header.Add("POLYMARKET-PROXY", "<api-key>")
    	req.Header.Add("POLYMARKET-SECRET", "<api-key>")
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.get("https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/account/orders")
      .header("POLYMARKET-PROXY", "<api-key>")
      .header("POLYMARKET-SECRET", "<api-key>")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://api.perpetuals.polymarket.com/v1/account/orders")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Get.new(url)
    request["POLYMARKET-PROXY"] = '<api-key>'
    request["POLYMARKET-SECRET"] = '<api-key>'
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

400

401

429

500

Request Weight:  
With order ID: **1**  
Without order ID: **10**

#### Authorizations

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#authorization-polymarket-proxy)

POLYMARKET-PROXY

string

header

required

Proxy address

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#authorization-polymarket-secret)

POLYMARKET-SECRET

string

header

required

Correponding proxy secret

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#parameter-order-id)

order\_id

integer

Order ID

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#parameter-client-order-id)

client\_order\_id

string

Client order ID

Required string length: `32`

Pattern: `^[0-9a-f]{32}$`

Example:

`"550e8400e29b41d4a716446655440000"`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#parameter-instrument-id)

instrument\_id

integer

Instrument ID

Example:

`1`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#parameter-start-timestamp)

start\_timestamp

integer

Start timestamp in milliseconds

Example:

`1767225600000`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#parameter-end-timestamp)

end\_timestamp

integer

End timestamp in milliseconds

Example:

`1767229200000`

#### Response

200

application/json

Orders response.

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#response-items-order-id)

order\_id

integer

required

Order ID

Example:

`1234567890`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#response-items-instrument-id)

instrument\_id

integer

required

Instrument ID

Example:

`1`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#response-items-buy)

buy

boolean

required

Is buy

Example:

`true`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#response-items-price)

price

string

required

Price

Example:

`"100.00"`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#response-items-quantity)

quantity

string

required

Quantity in no. of contracts

Example:

`"10.00"`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#response-items-tif)

tif

enum<string>

required

Time in force

Available options:

`gtc`,

`ioc`,

`fok`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#response-items-post-only)

post\_only

boolean

default:false

required

Post only

Example:

`false`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#response-items-ro)

ro

boolean

default:false

required

Reduce only

Example:

`false`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#response-items-resting-quantity)

resting\_quantity

string

required

Resting quantity

Example:

`"9.00"`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#response-items-filled-quantity)

filled\_quantity

string

required

Filled quantity

Example:

`"1.00"`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#response-items-status)

status

string

required

Order status

Example:

`"open"`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#response-items-created-timestamp)

created\_timestamp

integer

required

Create timestamp in milliseconds

Example:

`1767225600000`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#response-items-updated-timestamp)

updated\_timestamp

integer

required

Update timestamp in milliseconds

Example:

`1767225600000`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#response-items-client-order-id)

client\_order\_id

string

Client order ID

Required string length: `32`

Pattern: `^[0-9a-f]{32}$`

Example:

`"550e8400e29b41d4a716446655440000"`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/get-orders#response-items-tpsl)

tpsl

object

Conditional-order fields. Present only when the order is a TP/SL conditional order — `null`/omitted for regular orders.

# Rate Limits - Polymarket Documentation

> Documentation Index
> -------------------
> 
> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/llms.txt](https://docs.polymarket.com/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/rate-limits#content-area)

The limits on this page are IP-based and enforced using Cloudflare’s throttling system. When you exceed the limit for any endpoint, requests are throttled (delayed/queued) rather than immediately rejected. Limits reset on sliding time windows.

CLOB order and cancellation requests also use separate [per-signer token-bucket limits](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/trading-rate-limits)
.

* * *

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/rate-limits#general)

General
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------

| Endpoint | Limit |
| --- | --- |
| General rate limiting | 15,000 req / 10s |
| Health check (`/ok`) | 100 req / 10s |

* * *

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/rate-limits#gamma-api)

Gamma API
---------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Base URL: `https://gamma-api.polymarket.com`

| Endpoint | Limit |
| --- | --- |
| General | 4,000 req / 10s |
| `/events` | 500 req / 10s |
| `/markets` | 300 req / 10s |
| `/markets` + `/events` listing | 900 req / 10s |
| `/comments` | 200 req / 10s |
| `/tags` | 200 req / 10s |
| `/public-search` | 350 req / 10s |

* * *

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/rate-limits#data-api)

Data API
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Base URL: `https://data-api.polymarket.com`

### 

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/rate-limits#v1)

v1

| Endpoint | Limit |
| --- | --- |
| General | 1,000 req / 10s |
| `/trades` | 200 req / 10s |
| `/positions` | 150 req / 10s |
| `/closed-positions` | 150 req / 10s |
| Health check (`/ok`) | 100 req / 10s |

### 

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/rate-limits#v2)

v2

| Endpoint | Limit |
| --- | --- |
| General (all `/v2` endpoints) | 800 req / 10s |
| `/v2/trades` | 300 req / 10s |
| `/v2/positions`, `/v2/positions/combos` | 200 req / 10s |
| `/v2/activity`, `/v2/activity/combos` | 200 req / 10s |
| `/v2/prices-history` | 200 req / 10s |
| `/v2/status` | 100 req / 10s |

* * *

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/rate-limits#clob-api)

CLOB API
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Base URL: `https://clob.polymarket.com`

### 

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/rate-limits#general-2)

General

| Endpoint | Limit |
| --- | --- |
| General | 9,000 req / 10s |
| `GET` balance allowance | 200 req / 10s |
| `UPDATE` balance allowance | 50 req / 10s |

### 

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/rate-limits#market-data)

Market Data

| Endpoint | Limit |
| --- | --- |
| `/book` | 1,500 req / 10s |
| `/books` | 500 req / 10s |
| `/price` | 1,500 req / 10s |
| `/prices` | 500 req / 10s |
| `/midpoint` | 1,500 req / 10s |
| `/midpoints` | 500 req / 10s |
| `/prices-history` | 1,000 req / 10s |
| Market tick size | 200 req / 10s |

### 

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/rate-limits#ledger)

Ledger

| Endpoint | Limit |
| --- | --- |
| `/trades`, `/orders`, `/notifications`, `/order` | 900 req / 10s |
| `/data/orders` | 500 req / 10s |
| `/data/trades` | 500 req / 10s |
| `/notifications` | 125 req / 10s |

### 

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/rate-limits#authentication)

Authentication

| Endpoint | Limit |
| --- | --- |
| API key endpoints | 100 req / 10s |

### 

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/rate-limits#trading)

Trading

Cloudflare applies both **burst** limits (short spikes allowed) and **sustained** limits (longer-term average) to trading endpoints.

| Endpoint | Burst Limit | Sustained Limit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `POST /order` | 5,000 req / 10s | 120,000 req / 10 min |
| `DELETE /order` | 5,000 req / 10s | 120,000 req / 10 min |
| `POST /orders` | 2,000 req / 10s | 21,000 req / 10 min |
| `DELETE /orders` | 2,000 req / 10s | 15,000 req / 10 min |
| `DELETE /cancel-all` | 250 req / 10s | 6,000 req / 10 min |
| `DELETE /cancel-market-orders` | 1,500 req / 10s | 21,000 req / 10 min |

* * *

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/rate-limits#bridge-api)

Bridge API
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Base URL: `https://bridge.polymarket.com`

| Endpoint | Limit |
| --- | --- |
| General | 50 req / 10s |

* * *

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/rate-limits#other)

Other
-------------------------------------------------------------------------

| Endpoint | Limit |
| --- | --- |
| Relayer `/submit` | 25 req / 1 min |
| User PNL API | 200 req / 10s |

        "series": "<string>"\
      }\
    ]

#### Response

200 - application/json

List of sports metadata objects containing sport configuration details, visual assets, and related identifiers

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/sports/get-sports-metadata-information#response-items-sport)

sport

string

The sport identifier or abbreviation

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/sports/get-sports-metadata-information#response-items-image)

image

string<uri>

URL to the sport's logo or image asset

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/sports/get-sports-metadata-information#response-items-resolution)

resolution

string<uri>

URL to the official resolution source for the sport (e.g., league website)

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/sports/get-sports-metadata-information#response-items-ordering)

ordering

string

Preferred ordering for sport display, typically "home" or "away"

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/sports/get-sports-metadata-information#response-items-tags)

tags

string

Comma-separated list of tag IDs associated with the sport for categorization and filtering

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/sports/get-sports-metadata-information#response-items-series)

series

string

Series identifier linking the sport to a specific tournament or season series

Get market prices (query parameters)

cURL

    curl --request GET \
      --url https://clob.polymarket.com/prices

    import requests
    
    url = "https://clob.polymarket.com/prices"
    
    response = requests.get(url)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {method: 'GET'};
    
    fetch('https://clob.polymarket.com/prices', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://clob.polymarket.com/prices",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "GET",\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://clob.polymarket.com/prices"
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("GET", url, nil)
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.get("https://clob.polymarket.com/prices")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://clob.polymarket.com/prices")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Get.new(url)
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

200

Example

    {
      "0xabc123def456...": {
        "BUY": 0.45
      },
      "0xdef456abc123...": {
        "SELL": 0.52
      }

Get midpoint prices (request body)

cURL

    curl --request POST \
      --url https://clob.polymarket.com/midpoints \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '
    [\
      {\
        "token_id": "0xabc123def456..."\
      },\
      {\
        "token_id": "0xdef456abc123..."\
      }\
    ]
    '

    import requests
    
    url = "https://clob.polymarket.com/midpoints"
    
    payload = [{ "token_id": "0xabc123def456..." }, { "token_id": "0xdef456abc123..." }]
    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
    
    response = requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify([{token_id: '0xabc123def456...'}, {token_id: '0xdef456abc123...'}])
    };
    
    fetch('https://clob.polymarket.com/midpoints', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://clob.polymarket.com/midpoints",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "POST",\
      CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS => json_encode([\
        [\
            'token_id' => '0xabc123def456...'\
        ],\
        [\
            'token_id' => '0xdef456abc123...'\
        ]\
      ]),\
      CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => [\
        "Content-Type: application/json"\
      ],\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"strings"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://clob.polymarket.com/midpoints"
    
    	payload := strings.NewReader("[\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xabc123def456...\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xdef456abc123...\"\n  }\n]")
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", url, payload)
    
    	req.Header.Add("Content-Type", "application/json")
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.post("https://clob.polymarket.com/midpoints")
      .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
      .body("[\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xabc123def456...\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xdef456abc123...\"\n  }\n]")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://clob.polymarket.com/midpoints")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Post.new(url)
    request["Content-Type"] = 'application/json'
    request.body = "[\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xabc123def456...\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xdef456abc123...\"\n  }\n]"
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

# CLOB Trading Rate Limits - Polymarket Documentation

> Documentation Index
> -------------------
> 
> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/llms.txt](https://docs.polymarket.com/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/trading-rate-limits#content-area)

Polymarket evaluates CLOB order and cancellation requests against separate token buckets for each signer address. The signer address is the address associated with your CLOB API credentials. Activity in one bucket does not consume capacity from the other.

Beginning July 24, 2026, the limiter will run in warning mode for two weeks. Requests will continue to be processed during this period. A request that would be rejected after live enforcement begins will include `Poly-RateLimit-Warning: true`. Monitor this header and adjust your request patterns before live enforcement begins. Polymarket will announce when live enforcement begins.

These per-signer limits are separate from the existing [Cloudflare IP-based limits](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/rate-limits)
, which remain unchanged.

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/trading-rate-limits#how-rate-limits-work)

How Rate Limits Work
---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Each signer has an order bucket and a cancel bucket:

| Bucket | Request | Token Cost |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Order | `POST /order` | 1   |
| Order | `POST /orders` | Number of orders in a non-empty batch |
| Cancel | `DELETE /order` | 1   |
| Cancel | `DELETE /orders` | Number of submitted order IDs |
| Cancel | `DELETE /cancel-all` | 1 plus the number of orders canceled |
| Cancel | `DELETE /cancel-market-orders` | 1 plus the number of matching orders canceled |

### 

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/trading-rate-limits#refill-and-burst-capacity)

Refill and Burst Capacity

Tokens refill continuously at the rate assigned to the current tier. Burst capacity is the maximum number of tokens a bucket can hold. A full bucket can spend up to that capacity immediately; after the burst is consumed, new tokens become available at the refill rate. Calculate the duration of a full burst as:

    burst_seconds = burst / rate_per_sec
    

### 

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/trading-rate-limits#batch-requests)

Batch Requests

Under live enforcement, a batch is admitted only when the bucket contains enough tokens for every entry. Otherwise, the entire request is rejected and no entries are processed. This all-or-nothing rule applies to the rate-limit check; an admitted batch retains its normal per-order response behavior. A batch whose token cost exceeds the tier’s burst capacity can never be admitted as one request. Split it into smaller batches.

### 

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/trading-rate-limits#cancel-all-and-cancel-market-orders)

Cancel All and Cancel Market Orders

The number of orders that will be canceled is not known when `DELETE /cancel-all` or `DELETE /cancel-market-orders` begins. Each request first consumes one cancel token. After the cancellation result is known, the bucket is debited one additional token for every order successfully canceled. For tiers that allow a negative cancel balance, this second debit can put the bucket into debt. Future cancel requests remain blocked until the bucket has enough tokens for the next request. Other tiers floor the post-cancel balance at zero regardless of debit size.

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/trading-rate-limits#volume-tiers)

Volume Tiers
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Buckets are scoped to the signer address. Tier eligibility follows the cumulative trading volume of the maker wallet, even when it differs from the signer address. The volume window is currently 30 days, and tier assignments refresh every three hours.

| Tier | 30-Day Volume | Order Rate (tokens/s) | Order Burst (tokens) | Cancel Rate (tokens/s) | Cancel Burst (tokens) | Negative Cancel Balance |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Standard | —   | 40  | 60  | 80  | 120 | Yes |
| Copper | $30,000+ | 60  | 90  | 120 | 180 | Yes |
| Bronze | $50,000+ | 80  | 120 | 160 | 240 | Yes |
| Silver | $100,000+ | 200 | 300 | 400 | 600 | Yes |
| Gold | $500,000+ | 400 | 600 | 800 | 1,200 | Yes |
| Platinum | $2.5M+ | 450 | 675 | 900 | 1,350 | No  |
| Diamond | $5M+ | 525 | 787 | 1,050 | 1,575 | No  |
| Elite | $10M+ | 600 | 900 | 1,200 | 1,800 | No  |

Tier thresholds, the volume window, and rate limits may change as the system is tuned. Polymarket will provide advance notice of significant changes.

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/trading-rate-limits#response-headers)

Response Headers
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Responses include the following headers when the limiter successfully evaluates a covered request:

| Header | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `Poly-RateLimit-Remaining` | Current token balance for the applicable bucket after rate-limit accounting |
| `Poly-RateLimit-Reset` | Unix timestamp when the current rate-limit wait period ends |
| `Poly-RateLimit-Tier` | Rate-limit tier applied to the request |

The following headers appear only in specific cases:

| Header | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `Retry-After` | Retry delay in seconds; included on `429` responses |
| `Poly-RateLimit-Warning` | `true` in warning mode when live enforcement would have rejected the request |

`Poly-RateLimit-Reset` is not the time when the bucket will be completely full. On a `429` response for a request whose token cost fits within burst capacity, it indicates when enough tokens are expected for the attempted request. On an admitted request, it may be the current timestamp. `Poly-RateLimit-Remaining` can be negative after `DELETE /cancel-all` or `DELETE /cancel-market-orders` for tiers that allow a negative cancel balance. Before sending a large batch, compare its token cost with the remaining balance.

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/trading-rate-limits#handle-429-responses)

Handle 429 Responses
---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

After live enforcement begins, a request that requires more tokens than the applicable bucket contains returns `429 Too Many Requests`. For a request whose token cost is within the tier’s burst capacity, `Retry-After` gives the minimum delay before retrying. You do not need to wait for the bucket to refill completely. If the request costs more than the burst capacity, split it into smaller requests instead of retrying it unchanged.

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/trading-rate-limits#get-help)

Get Help
---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Contact [support@polymarket.com](mailto:support@polymarket.com)
 if you have questions about your tier, believe your wallet qualifies for a higher tier, recently moved trading activity to a new address, or believe you are being limited incorrectly.

YesNo

[Skip to main content](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/market-data/get-fee-rate#content-area)

Get fee rate

cURL

    curl --request GET \
      --url https://clob.polymarket.com/fee-rate

    import requests
    
    url = "https://clob.polymarket.com/fee-rate"
    
    response = requests.get(url)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {method: 'GET'};
    
    fetch('https://clob.polymarket.com/fee-rate', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://clob.polymarket.com/fee-rate",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "GET",\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://clob.polymarket.com/fee-rate"
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("GET", url, nil)
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.get("https://clob.polymarket.com/fee-rate")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://clob.polymarket.com/fee-rate")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Get.new(url)
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

# Get last trade prices (request body) - Polymarket Documentation

> Documentation Index
> -------------------
> 
> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/llms.txt](https://docs.polymarket.com/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/market-data/get-last-trade-prices-request-body#content-area)

Get last trade prices (request body)

cURL

    curl --request POST \
      --url https://clob.polymarket.com/last-trades-prices \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '
    [\
      {\
        "token_id": "0xabc123def456..."\
      },\
      {\
        "token_id": "0xdef456abc123..."\
      }\
    ]
    '

    import requests
    
    url = "https://clob.polymarket.com/last-trades-prices"
    
    payload = [{ "token_id": "0xabc123def456..." }, { "token_id": "0xdef456abc123..." }]
    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
    
    response = requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify([{token_id: '0xabc123def456...'}, {token_id: '0xdef456abc123...'}])
    };
    
    fetch('https://clob.polymarket.com/last-trades-prices', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://clob.polymarket.com/last-trades-prices",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "POST",\
      CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS => json_encode([\
        [\
            'token_id' => '0xabc123def456...'\
        ],\
        [\
            'token_id' => '0xdef456abc123...'\
        ]\
      ]),\
      CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => [\
        "Content-Type: application/json"\
      ],\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"strings"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://clob.polymarket.com/last-trades-prices"
    
    	payload := strings.NewReader("[\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xabc123def456...\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xdef456abc123...\"\n  }\n]")
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", url, payload)
    
    	req.Header.Add("Content-Type", "application/json")
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.post("https://clob.polymarket.com/last-trades-prices")
      .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
      .body("[\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xabc123def456...\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xdef456abc123...\"\n  }\n]")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://clob.polymarket.com/last-trades-prices")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Post.new(url)
    request["Content-Type"] = 'application/json'
    request.body = "[\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xabc123def456...\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xdef456abc123...\"\n  }\n]"
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

200

Example

    [\
      {\
        "token_id": "0xabc123def456...",\
        "price": "0.45",\
        "side": "BUY"\
      },\
      {\
        "token_id": "0xdef456abc123...",\
        "price": "0.52",\
        "side": "SELL"\
      }\
    ]

[Skip to main content](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/market-data/get-market-prices-request-body#content-area)

Get market prices (request body)

cURL

    curl --request POST \
      --url https://clob.polymarket.com/prices \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '
    [\
      {\
        "token_id": "0xabc123def456...",\
        "side": "BUY"\
      },\
      {\
        "token_id": "0xdef456abc123...",\
        "side": "SELL"\
      }\
    ]
    '

    import requests
    
    url = "https://clob.polymarket.com/prices"
    
    payload = [\
        {\
            "token_id": "0xabc123def456...",\
            "side": "BUY"\
        },\
        {\
            "token_id": "0xdef456abc123...",\
            "side": "SELL"\
        }\
    ]
    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
    
    response = requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify([\
        {token_id: '0xabc123def456...', side: 'BUY'},\
        {token_id: '0xdef456abc123...', side: 'SELL'}\
      ])
    };
    
    fetch('https://clob.polymarket.com/prices', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://clob.polymarket.com/prices",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "POST",\
      CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS => json_encode([\
        [\
            'token_id' => '0xabc123def456...',\
            'side' => 'BUY'\
        ],\
        [\
            'token_id' => '0xdef456abc123...',\
            'side' => 'SELL'\
        ]\
      ]),\
      CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => [\
        "Content-Type: application/json"\
      ],\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"strings"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://clob.polymarket.com/prices"
    
    	payload := strings.NewReader("[\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xabc123def456...\",\n    \"side\": \"BUY\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xdef456abc123...\",\n    \"side\": \"SELL\"\n  }\n]")
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", url, payload)
    
    	req.Header.Add("Content-Type", "application/json")
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.post("https://clob.polymarket.com/prices")
      .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
      .body("[\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xabc123def456...\",\n    \"side\": \"BUY\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xdef456abc123...\",\n    \"side\": \"SELL\"\n  }\n]")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://clob.polymarket.com/prices")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Post.new(url)
    request["Content-Type"] = 'application/json'
    request.body = "[\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xabc123def456...\",\n    \"side\": \"BUY\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xdef456abc123...\",\n    \"side\": \"SELL\"\n  }\n]"
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

200

Example

    {
      "0xabc123def456...": {
        "BUY": 0.45
      },
      "0xdef456abc123...": {
        "SELL": 0.52
      }
    }

Get spreads

cURL

    curl --request POST \
      --url https://clob.polymarket.com/spreads \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '
    [\
      {\
        "token_id": "0xabc123def456..."\
      },\
      {\
        "token_id": "0xdef456abc123..."\
      }\
    ]
    '

    import requests
    
    url = "https://clob.polymarket.com/spreads"
    
    payload = [{ "token_id": "0xabc123def456..." }, { "token_id": "0xdef456abc123..." }]
    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
    
    response = requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify([{token_id: '0xabc123def456...'}, {token_id: '0xdef456abc123...'}])
    };
    
    fetch('https://clob.polymarket.com/spreads', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://clob.polymarket.com/spreads",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "POST",\
      CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS => json_encode([\
        [\
            'token_id' => '0xabc123def456...'\
        ],\
        [\
            'token_id' => '0xdef456abc123...'\
        ]\
      ]),\
      CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => [\
        "Content-Type: application/json"\
      ],\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"strings"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://clob.polymarket.com/spreads"
    
    	payload := strings.NewReader("[\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xabc123def456...\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xdef456abc123...\"\n  }\n]")
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", url, payload)
    
    	req.Header.Add("Content-Type", "application/json")
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.post("https://clob.polymarket.com/spreads")
      .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
      .body("[\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xabc123def456...\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xdef456abc123...\"\n  }\n]")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://clob.polymarket.com/spreads")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Post.new(url)
    request["Content-Type"] = 'application/json'
    request.body = "[\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xabc123def456...\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"token_id\": \"0xdef456abc123...\"\n  }\n]"
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

market

required

startTs

number<double>

Filter by items after this unix timestamp.

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/markets/get-prices-history#parameter-end-ts)

endTs

number<double>

Filter by items before this unix timestamp.

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/markets/get-prices-history#parameter-interval)

interval

enum<string>

Time interval for data aggregation.

Available options:

`max`,

`all`,

`1m`,

`1w`,

`1d`,

`6h`,

`1h`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/markets/get-prices-history#parameter-fidelity)

fidelity

integer

Accuracy of the data expressed in minutes. Default is 1 minute.

user

required

200

application/zip

The response is of type `file`.

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/relayer/get-relayer-address-and-nonce#parameter-address)

address

string

required

User's signer address Ethereum address (0x-prefixed, 40 hex chars)

Pattern: `^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}$`

Example:

`"0x6e0c80c90ea6c15917308F820Eac91Ce2724B5b5"`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/relayer/get-relayer-address-and-nonce#parameter-type)

type

enum<string>

required

Type of nonce to retrieve

Available options:

`PROXY`,

`SAFE`

#### Response

200

application/json

Relay payload retrieved successfully

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/relayer/get-relayer-address-and-nonce#response-address)

address

string

Relayer address

Pattern: `^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}$`

Example:

`"0x4da9395388791c22684e03779c3de10934eb9cfb"`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/relayer/get-relayer-address-and-nonce#response-nonce)

nonce

string

Current nonce value

Example:

`"31"`

        "price": "0.45",\
        "side": "BUY"\
      },\
      {\
        "token_id": "0xdef456abc123...",\
        "price": "0.52",\
        "side": "SELL"\
      }\
    ]

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/market-data/get-last-trade-prices-query-parameters#parameter-token-ids)

token\_ids

string

required

Comma-separated list of token IDs (max 500)

#### Response

200

application/json

Successfully retrieved last trade prices

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/market-data/get-last-trade-prices-query-parameters#response-items-token-id)

token\_id

string

required

Token ID (asset ID)

Example:

`"0xabc123def456..."`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/market-data/get-last-trade-prices-query-parameters#response-items-price)

price

string

required

Last trade price

Example:

`"0.45"`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/market-data/get-last-trade-prices-query-parameters#response-items-side)

side

enum<string>

required

Last trade side (BUY or SELL)

Available options:

`BUY`,

`SELL`

Example:

Get tick size

cURL

    curl --request GET \
      --url https://clob.polymarket.com/tick-size

    import requests
    
    url = "https://clob.polymarket.com/tick-size"
    
    response = requests.get(url)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {method: 'GET'};
    
    fetch('https://clob.polymarket.com/tick-size', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://clob.polymarket.com/tick-size",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "GET",\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://clob.polymarket.com/tick-size"
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("GET", url, nil)
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.get("https://clob.polymarket.com/tick-size")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://clob.polymarket.com/tick-size")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Get.new(url)
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

# Get batch prices history - Polymarket Documentation

> Documentation Index
> -------------------
> 
> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/llms.txt](https://docs.polymarket.com/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/markets/get-batch-prices-history#content-area)

Get batch prices history

cURL

    curl --request POST \
      --url https://clob.polymarket.com/batch-prices-history \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --data '
    {
      "markets": [\
        "<string>"\
      ],
      "start_ts": 123,
      "end_ts": 123,
      "fidelity": 123
    }
    '

    import requests
    
    url = "https://clob.polymarket.com/batch-prices-history"
    
    payload = {
        "markets": ["<string>"],
        "start_ts": 123,
        "end_ts": 123,
        "fidelity": 123
    }
    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
    
    response = requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({markets: ['<string>'], start_ts: 123, end_ts: 123, fidelity: 123})
    };
    
    fetch('https://clob.polymarket.com/batch-prices-history', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://clob.polymarket.com/batch-prices-history",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "POST",\
      CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS => json_encode([\
        'markets' => [\
            '<string>'\
        ],\
        'start_ts' => 123,\
        'end_ts' => 123,\
        'fidelity' => 123\
      ]),\
      CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => [\
        "Content-Type: application/json"\
      ],\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"strings"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://clob.polymarket.com/batch-prices-history"
    
    	payload := strings.NewReader("{\n  \"markets\": [\n    \"<string>\"\n  ],\n  \"start_ts\": 123,\n  \"end_ts\": 123,\n  \"fidelity\": 123\n}")
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", url, payload)
    
    	req.Header.Add("Content-Type", "application/json")
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.post("https://clob.polymarket.com/batch-prices-history")
      .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
      .body("{\n  \"markets\": [\n    \"<string>\"\n  ],\n  \"start_ts\": 123,\n  \"end_ts\": 123,\n  \"fidelity\": 123\n}")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://clob.polymarket.com/batch-prices-history")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Post.new(url)
    request["Content-Type"] = 'application/json'
    request.body = "{\n  \"markets\": [\n    \"<string>\"\n  ],\n  \"start_ts\": 123,\n  \"end_ts\": 123,\n  \"fidelity\": 123\n}"
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

200

400

500

    {
      "history": {}
    }

    {
      "error": "<string>",
      "code": "<string>",
      "retry_after_seconds": 123
    }

    {
      "error": "<string>",
      "code": "<string>",
      "retry_after_seconds": 123
    }

#### Body

application/json

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/markets/get-batch-prices-history#body-markets)

markets

string\[\]

required

List of market asset ids to query. Maximum 20.

Maximum array length: `20`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/markets/get-batch-prices-history#body-start-ts)

start\_ts

number<double>

Filter by items after this unix timestamp (seconds).

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/markets/get-batch-prices-history#body-end-ts)

end\_ts

number<double>

Filter by items before this unix timestamp (seconds).

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/markets/get-batch-prices-history#body-interval)

interval

enum<string>

Time interval for data aggregation.

Available options:

`max`,

`all`,

`1m`,

`1w`,

`1d`,

`6h`,

`1h`

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/markets/get-batch-prices-history#body-fidelity)

fidelity

integer

Accuracy of the data expressed in minutes. Default is 1 minute.

#### Response

200

application/json

Successful response with price history for each market

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/markets/get-batch-prices-history#response-history)

history

object

Map of market asset id to array of price data points.

Show child attributes

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/misc/get-total-markets-a-user-has-traded#parameter-user)

user

string

required

User Profile Address (0x-prefixed, 40 hex chars)

Pattern: `^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}$`

Example:

`"0x56687bf447db6ffa42ffe2204a05edaa20f55839"`

#### Response

200

application/json

Success

[​](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/misc/get-total-markets-a-user-has-traded#response-user)
